- **figma-from-code-validate**: Subagent for figma-from-code Phase 5. Validates the completed Figma rebuild by comparing assembled screen frames against app screenshots. - **figma-from-code-validator**: Standalone validator for a completed figma-from-code rebuild. Also used by the Phase 5 orchestrator agent as a reference for the Component App Map and comparison scripts.
